As the Republican People's Party, we have shown maximum effort to make the law as positive as possible in terms of environment and human rights as possible in terms of natural rights. We'd like to vote yes to this law, but we're not completely satisfied with some of our reservations about the Environment Agency in particular. Despite everything, the Republican People's Party has become much more positive than it was when it came to the initiatives of its members of the Commission, which we accept, but because of our reservations about the Environment Agency, I pledge to the law that we will vote no. My respects.
